Delegate to it for anything visual: "design this", "polish this screen", "is this accessible", "critique this", "prep the hand-off", "extract a design system". It produces designs, specs, and reviews inside the harness's gates — it does not implement production code (that's the engineer) and does not write product copy (that's the copywriter). ([beeuxd/bee-design-harness](https://github.com/beeuxd/bee-design-harness/blob/HEAD/role-designer/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: Thin design orchestrator. Routes to design-method-finder, product-designer, microinteractions-expert, optional impeccable, or critique/fallback. ([Benjamin-Howard-IBM/Benjaminhoward-Pocket-Product-Designer](https://github.com/Benjamin-Howard-IBM/Benjaminhoward-Pocket-Product-Designer/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: Use this agent for UI/UX design decisions: layout hierarchy, visual rhythm, color contrast, component composition, interaction states, motion. Produces design specs (not code) that frontend-dev implements. Use BEFORE frontend-dev when a change has non-obvious design choices; use AFTER frontend-dev to audit visual polish. ([Benjamin-Seiberl/KVWN](https://github.com/Benjamin-Seiberl/KVWN/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/designer.md))
- **designer** ([bgutsev/desk-flight-radar](https://github.com/bgutsev/desk-flight-radar/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: 차량 인포테인먼트 UI/UX 디자이너. 정의 단계에서는 화면 명세·사용자 흐름·디자인 토큰·시선 예산표를 docs/design/에 쓰고(PM 호출), 실행 단계에서는 통합 후 에뮬레이터 스크린샷을 운전자 주의분산 기준으로 검수한다(orchestrator 호출). 코드는 쓰지 않는다. ([biyott/pleos-companion-mono](https://github.com/biyott/pleos-companion-mono/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/designer.md) · 2 copies)
- **Designer**: UI/UX design specialist — mockups, components, styling, visual work ([bketelsen/max](https://github.com/bketelsen/max/blob/HEAD/agents/designer.agent.md))
- **designer**: UX/UI architect проекта. Пишет ТЗ (техзадание) для frontend-specialist в едином стиле — макет, компоненты, состояния, копирайт RU. Use proactively ПЕРЕД любой UI-задачей (новая страница, новый компонент, существенный редизайн). НЕ пишет код. ([Bogdan-cool-coder/MGCRM](https://github.com/Bogdan-cool-coder/MGCRM/blob/HEAD/examples/contracts/.claude/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: UX/UI-архитектор MACRO Global CRM. Пишет markdown-ТЗ (техзадание) для frontend-specialist в едином стиле — макет, PrimeVue-компоненты, состояния, копирайт RU(+EN). Использовать ПЕРЕД любой UI-задачей (новая страница, новый компонент, существенный редизайн). НЕ пишет код (Write — только .md ТЗ). - **designer**: Judges and improves how a page looks and reads — hierarchy, spacing, type, color, motion, and copy — against the brand in CLAUDE.md and the tokens in globals.css.
